+// Initialize all three fields in parallel.  Besides generating the initial
+// concentration, this gives each array a NUMA-friendly parallel first touch.
+void initializeFields(double* const concentration, double* const next,
+                      double* const chemicalPotential, const size_t volume) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < volume; ++i) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
+                              static_cast<double>(volume);
+        concentration[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+        next[i] = 0.0;
+        chemicalPotential[i] = 0.0;
+    }
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = mu + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = mu + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = mu + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = mu + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = mu + plusZBase;
+                    const double* const old = current + base;
+                    double* const output = next + base;
+
+                    const auto updateAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                              const size_t xMinus,
+                                              const size_t xPlus) {
+                        output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                    };
+
+                    updateAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * (dxx + dyy + dzz);
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        updateAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);

+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = mu + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = mu + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = mu + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = mu + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = mu + plusZBase;
+                    const double* const old = current + base;
+                    double* const output = next + base;
+
+                    const auto updateAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                              const size_t xMinus,
+                                              const size_t xPlus) {
+                        output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                    };

+                    updateAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {

+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {

+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * (dxx + dyy + dzz);
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        updateAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }

project(cahn_hilliard_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)

# Enable optimizations

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);
 }

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
-    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
-    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;

-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);

+// Initialize all three fields in parallel.  Besides generating the initial
+// concentration, this gives each array a NUMA-friendly parallel first touch.
+void initializeFields(double* const concentration, double* const next,
+                      double* const chemicalPotential, const size_t volume) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < volume; ++i) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
+                              static_cast<double>(volume);
+        concentration[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+        next[i] = 0.0;
+        chemicalPotential[i] = 0.0;
+    }
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}

-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,

-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = mu + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = mu + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = mu + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = mu + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = mu + plusZBase;
+                    const double* const old = current + base;
+                    double* const output = next + base;
+
+                    const auto updateAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                              const size_t xMinus,
+                                              const size_t xPlus) {
+                        output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                    };

+                    updateAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);

+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =

+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * (dxx + dyy + dzz);
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        updateAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }

             }

-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
-    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
-    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;

-    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);

+// Initialize all three fields in parallel.  Besides generating the initial
+// concentration, this gives each array a NUMA-friendly parallel first touch.
+void initializeFields(double* const concentration, double* const next,
+                      double* const chemicalPotential, const size_t volume) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < volume; ++i) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
+                              static_cast<double>(volume);
+        concentration[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+        next[i] = 0.0;
+        chemicalPotential[i] = 0.0;
+    }
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = mu + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = mu + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = mu + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = mu + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = mu + plusZBase;
+                    const double* const old = current + base;
+                    double* const output = next + base;
+
+                    const auto updateAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                              const size_t xMinus,
+                                              const size_t xPlus) {
+                        output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                    };
+
+                    updateAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * (dxx + dyy + dzz);
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        updateAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);

+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,

+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}

+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;

+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);

+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;

+                            output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * (dxx + dyy + dzz);
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        updateAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }

             }

+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);

-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);
 }

+                      double* const chemicalPotential, const size_t volume) {

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {

-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +

+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }

+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }

I restored the original floating-point operation order, so the optimized executable now matches the serial reference bit-for-bit—including the result hash—across irregular grids and 1, 2, 8, and 32 threads. The stencil loops are confirmed to vectorize with AVX2 on this host; I’m doing the final clean build and scaling check now.

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);

+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D *

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);

+void initializeFields(double* const concentration, double* const next,
+                      double* const chemicalPotential, const size_t volume) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = mu + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = mu + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = mu + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = mu + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = mu + plusZBase;
+                    const double* const old = current + base;
+                    double* const output = next + base;
+
+                    const auto updateAt = [&](const size_t x,

codex
Implemented OpenMP parallelization in:

- [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:33)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:7)

Key improvements:

- Persistent OpenMP team across all time steps.
- Parallel static row partitioning and SIMD-vectorized stencil loops.
- NUMA-friendly parallel first-touch with 64-byte-aligned arrays.
- Required OpenMP build dependency; executable remains `cahn_hilliard`.
- Bitwise-identical results and hashes versus the original across tested grids and 1–32 threads.

Validation passed. On a 320³ grid, performance scaled from 275 MCellUpdates/s with one thread to 6,307 MCellUpdates/s with 64 threads—approximately 23×.

+using AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<double, decltype(&std::free)>;
+
+AlignedArray allocateArray(const size_t count) {
+    if (count == 0 || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+
+    void* allocation = nullptr;
+    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;
+    if (posix_memalign(&allocation, alignment, count * sizeof(double)) != 0) {
+        throw std::bad_alloc();
+    }
+    return AlignedArray(static_cast<double*>(allocation), &std::free);
 }

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {

-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;

+// Initialize all three fields in parallel.  Besides generating the initial
+// concentration, this gives each array a NUMA-friendly parallel first touch.
+void initializeFields(double* const concentration, double* const next,
+                      double* const chemicalPotential, const size_t volume) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < volume; ++i) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
+                              static_cast<double>(volume);

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+inline double laplacianPoint(const double* const row,
+                             const double* const rowMinusY,
+                             const double* const rowPlusY,
+                             const double* const rowMinusZ,
+                             const double* const rowPlusZ,
+                             const size_t x, const size_t xMinus,
+                             const size_t xPlus, const double invDx2,
+                             const double invDy2, const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double dxx = (row[xPlus] + row[xMinus] - 2.0 * center) * invDx2;
+    const double dyy = (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDy2;
+    const double dzz = (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * center) * invDz2;
+    return dxx + dyy + dzz;
+}
+
+// Run both stencils in one persistent OpenMP region.  The implicit barrier at
+// the end of each worksharing loop is required: the update needs every value
+// of mu, and the following time step needs every value of the updated field.
+double* runSimulation(double* const cold, double* const cnew, double* const mu,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                      const int iterations, const double D, const double dt,
+                      const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                      const double gamma, const double eAA,
+                      const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    if (iterations <= 0) {
+        return cold;
+    }
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double updateScale = dt * D;
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
+            const double* const current = (t & 1) == 0 ? cold : cnew;
+            double* const next = (t & 1) == 0 ? cnew : cold;
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = current + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = current + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = current + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = current + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = current + plusZBase;
+                    double* const output = mu + base;
+
+                    const auto chemicalPotentialAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                                         const size_t xMinus,
+                                                         const size_t xPlus) {
+                        const double value = row[x];
+                        const double laplacian = laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
+                        output[x] =
+                            4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                   (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                   2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                            3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                            gamma * laplacian;
+                    };
+
+                    chemicalPotentialAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);
+
+                    if (nx > 2) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                            const double value = row[x];
+                            const double dxx =
+                                (row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            const double laplacian = dxx + dyy + dzz;
+                            output[x] =
+                                4.5 * ((value + 1.0) * eAA +
+                                       (value - 1.0) * eBB -
+                                       2.0 * value * eAB) +
+                                3.0 * value + value * value * value -
+                                gamma * laplacian;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        chemicalPotentialAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }
-    }
-}
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+                    const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+                    const size_t minusYBase = y == 0 ? base : base - nx;
+                    const size_t plusYBase = y + 1 == ny ? base : base + nx;
+                    const size_t minusZBase = z == 0 ? base : base - plane;
+                    const size_t plusZBase = z + 1 == nz ? base : base + plane;
+
+                    const double* const row = mu + base;
+                    const double* const rowMinusY = mu + minusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusY = mu + plusYBase;
+                    const double* const rowMinusZ = mu + minusZBase;
+                    const double* const rowPlusZ = mu + plusZBase;
+                    const double* const old = current + base;
+                    double* const output = next + base;
+
+                    const auto updateAt = [&](const size_t x,
+                                              const size_t xMinus,
+                                              const size_t xPlus) {
+                        output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * laplacianPoint(
+                            row, rowMinusY, rowPlusY, rowMinusZ, rowPlusZ,
+                            x, xMinus, xPlus, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);

+                    updateAt(0, 0, nx == 1 ? 0 : 1);

+                            const double dyy =
+                                (rowPlusY[x] + rowMinusY[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+                            const double dzz =
+                                (rowPlusZ[x] + rowMinusZ[x] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+                            output[x] = old[x] + updateScale * (dxx + dyy + dzz);
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (nx > 1) {
+                        updateAt(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+                    }
+                }
             }
-        }

             }

Implemented OpenMP parallelization in:

- [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:33)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:7)

Key improvements:

- Persistent OpenMP team across all time steps.
- Parallel static row partitioning and SIMD-vectorized stencil loops.
- NUMA-friendly parallel first-touch with 64-byte-aligned arrays.
- Required OpenMP build dependency; executable remains `cahn_hilliard`.
- Bitwise-identical results and hashes versus the original across tested grids and 1–32 threads.

Validation passed. On a 320³ grid, performance scaled from 275 MCellUpdates/s with one thread to 6,307 MCellUpdates/s with 64 threads—approximately 23×.
